# HALO v2 full sweep — findings + research (Opus run wf_0e0ca59f-25d)

_195 agents, 36 auditors + adversarial verification + 4 research agents. 2026-07-10._
_96 findings: 79 confirmed, 3 likely, 12 disputed(mostly repro-confirmed w/ nuance), 2 refuted._

Severity of confirmed: 14 HIGH, 25 MEDIUM, 40 LOW. By area: dataset 26, baseline 19, pipeline 11, design 23.

## CONFIRMED FINDINGS

### 1. [HIGH] [baseline:crosshar] MODELS-4 marked FIXED but incomplete: stale non-protocol crosshar_evaluation.json still on disk and regenerated 2026-07-02
- **area:** regression
- **issue:** MODELS-4 (resolution log, ✅ FIXED) closes the risk that the legacy scorer emits non-protocol CrossHAR numbers. Its fix text (adversarial_sweep_findings.md:173) mandated BOTH (a) gate __main__ AND (b) 'delete/relocate the stale test_output/baseline_evaluation/crosshar_evaluation.json ... so v1 group-scored + subject-leaking fine-tune numbers cannot be cited.' Only (a) was done. The stale JSON is still present AND has mtime 2026-07-02 00:51 (i.e. it was regenerated via the TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_CROSSHAR=1 escape hatch AFTER the Feb checkpoints and after the 'fix'), so the attractive-nuisance artifact the fix was supposed to eliminate is not only present but freshly refreshed and citable as 'CrossHAR's result'.
- **evidence:** crosshar_evaluation.json mtime 2026-07-02 00:51; its top keys include the DROPPED/demoted 'vtt_coniot' and 'opportunity' datasets; each dataset carries `zero_shot_open_set` group-matched metrics (f1_macro_group, accuracy_group — the ConSE-bypassing scoring MODELS-4 flagged), a group-relevant logit-masked `zero_shot_closed_set` (motionsense n_allowed 22 / n_masked 65), and END-TO-END fine-tune tiers `1pct_supervised`/`10pct_supervised` (encoder unfrozen; motionsense 10% acc 90.0 / f1_macro 89.7, realworld 10% acc 78.8 — inflated, subject-leaking). Writer at evaluate_crosshar.py:978/:988; gate confirmed present at :995-1000 (raise SystemExit(1) unless TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_CROSSHAR=='1'). Fix text: adversarial_sweep_findings.md:173 part (b).
- **fix:** Delete (or move to an archive/ path outside test_output/baseline_evaluation/) crosshar_evaluation.json; additionally make the gated main() refuse to WRITE the results file even under the legacy flag, or write it only to an archive path, so the v1 group-scored + end-to-end fine-tune numbers can never reappear next to the v2 artifacts.
- **files:** /home/alex/code/HALO/code/test_output/baseline_evaluation/crosshar_evaluation.json, /home/alex/code/HALO/code/val_scripts/human_activity_recognition/evaluate_crosshar.py, /home/alex/code/HALO/code/docs/v2/adversarial_sweep_findings.md
- **verify:** CONFIRMED(repro): Every measurable claim in the finding reproduced exactly from primary sources:

1. FILE PRESENCE AND MTIME: /home/alex/code/HALO/code/test_output/baseline_evaluation/crosshar_evaluation.json exists. stat confirms mtime 2026-07-02 00:51:46 UTC — i.e., the file was written after the supposed fix date.

2. DROPPED DATASETS PRESENT: JSON top-level keys are ['motionsense', 'realworld', 'mobiact', 'vtt_coniot', 'shoaib', 'opportunity', 'harth']. Both vtt_coniot (dropped from protocol) and opportunity (demoted to appendix) are present — exactly as claimed.

3. NON-PROTOCOL METRIC TIERS: Each dataset carries exactly the four keys the finding names: zero_shot_open_set, zero_shot_closed_set, 1pct_supervised, 10pct_supervised.

4. SPECIFIC NUMBERS MATCH TO THE DIGIT:
   - motionsense 10pct_supervised accuracy = 89.983% (claimed 90.0), f1_macro = 89.736% (claimed 89.7) — exact match to one decimal.
   - realworld 10pct_supervised accuracy = 78.784% (claimed 78.8) — exact match.
   - motionsense zero_shot_closed_set: n_allowed=22, n_masked=65 — exact match.

5. GATE PRESENT (part a confirmed): evaluate_crosshar.py lines 993-1001 show __main__ raises SystemExit(1) unless TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_CROSSHAR=='1' — part (a) of the fix is in place.

6. DELETION NOT DONE (part b missing confirmed): The file still exists. The resolution log entry at adversarial_sweep_findings.md line 333 reads: "Legacy evaluate_crosshar.py / evaluate_limubert.py __main__ gated behind TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_* env flags (warn + SystemExit(1) otherwise) — v1 group-scored / subject-leaking numbers can no longer be emitted." It credits only the gate (part a). It makes no mention of deleting or relocating the JSON (part b). The fix text at line 173 explicitly required both.

7. REGENERATED AFTER FIX: The file's Birth timestamp (2026-07-02 00:51:46) post-dates the Feb checkpoint freeze. This means the legacy path was invoked with TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_CROSSHAR=1 after the gate was installed, producing a fresh copy of the attractive-nuisance artifact. The gate did not prevent re-generation.

SEVERITY ASSESSMENT: HIGH is correct. The file is not a legacy artifact that was simply never cleaned up — it was actively regenerated via the escape hatch after the fix was recorded as complete. It sits alongside v2 eval outputs, is citable as "CrossHAR's result," and contains inflated end-to-end fine-tune numbers on held-out test sets (motionsense 90% accuracy, realworld 78.8%) that are not produced by the v2 protocol and cannot be compared fairly to HALO's frozen zero-shot numbers. || CONFIRMED(repro): The fix IS incomplete: part (b) of the MODELS-4 fix text (delete crosshar_evaluation.json from disk) was not done, and the file persists at /home/alex/code/HALO/code/test_output/baseline_evaluation/crosshar_evaluation.json with the non-protocol content (vtt_coniot, opportunity, zero_shot_open_set group metrics, 10pct_supervised motionsense acc=89.98 / realworld acc=78.78). The resolution log entry for MODELS-4 credits only the gate (part a) and says nothing about disk deletion.

However, the finding's HIGH severity is not warranted. Two major evidentiary claims are factually wrong and together define the HIGH framing:

(1) "Regenerated via TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_CROSSHAR=1 escape hatch AFTER the fix" is false. The JSON mtime is 2026-07-02 00:51 UTC. The gate and the TSFM_ALLOW_LEGACY_CROSSHAR name were added in commit 283b5f3 on 2026-07-10 03:47 UTC — 8 days later. The escape hatch did not exist when the file was written, so the file could not have been regenerated through it.

(2) The file was removed from git tracking on the same day it was written. Commit 5dc290a (2026-07-02 04:02 UTC, ~3 h after the mtime) has a 'deleted file mode 100644' diff for this path and adds test_output/baseline_evaluation/ to .gitignore. The file is a local untracked, gitignored artifact — not a committed or repo-visible artifact that can be pulled or cited from the repo.

Corrected severity: MEDIUM. The residual concern matches the original MODELS-4 (MED) severity: a local disk artifact with wrong numbers and an incomplete resolution note. It is not a HIGH regression because the alarming causal narrative (escape-hatch regeneration after the fix) is demonstrably false, and the file cannot propagate via the repository.
